exact_match_rate, voting_confidence\\nMetrics: Spearman rho, Kendall tau, AUROC (for NER and RE separately)\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom scipy.stats import spearmanr, kendalltau\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/code\\\")\\n\\nfrom consistency import compute_all_consistency_scores\\nfrom evaluation import per_instance_f1\\n\\n\\ndef load_jsonl(path):\\n    instances = []\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            if line.strip():\\n                instances.append(json.loads(line))\\n    return instances\\n\\n\\ndef auroc(scores, labels):\\n    scores = np.array(scores, dtype=float)\\n    labels = np.array(labels, dtype=int)\\n    if len(set(labels)) < 2:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    pos = scores[labels == 1]\\n    neg = scores[labels == 0]\\n    if len(pos) == 0 or len(neg) == 0:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    count = sum(\\n        float(np.sum(p > neg) + 0.5 * np.sum(p == neg)) for p in pos\\n    )\\n    return count / (len(pos) * len(neg))\\n\\n\\ndef _entity_set_key(sample):\\n    return frozenset((e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"]) for e in sample.get(\\\"entities\\\", []))\\n\\ndef _relation_set_key(sample):\\n    return frozenset((r[\\\"head\\\"], r[\\\"tail\\\"], r[\\\"type\\\"]) for r in sample.get(\\\"relations\\\", []))\\n\\ndef exact_match_rate(samples, subtask):\\n    if not samples:\\n        return 0.0\\n    key_fn = _entity_set_key if subtask == \\\"ner\\\" else _relation_set_key\\n    keys = [key_fn(s) for s in samples]\\n    counter = Counter(keys)\\n    return counter.most_common(1)[0][1] / len(samples)\\n\\n\\ndef voting_confidence_ner(samples):\\n    N = len(samples)\\n    if N == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    counter = Counter()\\n    for s in samples:\\n        for e in s.get(\\\"entities\\\", []):\\n            counter[(e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"])] += 1\\n    if not counter:\\n        return 0.0\\n    return float(np.mean([c / N for c in counter.values()]))\\n\\ndef voting_confidence_re(samples):\\n    N = len(samples)\\n    if N == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    counter = Counter()\\n    for s in samples:\\n        for r in s.get(\\\"relations\\\", []):\\n            counter[(r[\\\"head\\\"], r[\\\"tail\\\"], r[\\\"type\\\"])] += 1\\n    if not counter:\\n        return 0.0\\n    return float(np.mean([c / N for c in counter.values()]))\\n\\n\\ndef compute_all_signals(instances, subtask):\\n    consistency = compute_all_consistency_scores(instances, subtask=subtask)\\n    sj = consistency[\\\"soft_jaccard\\\"]\\n    fk = consistency[\\\"fleiss_kappa\\\"]\\n\\n    em_rates = []\\n    v_confs = []\\n    logprobs = []\\n    greedy_f1s = []\\n\\n    vote_fn = voting_confidence_ner if subtask == \\\"ner\\\" else voting_confidence_re\\n\\n    for inst in instances:\\n        em_rates.append(exact_match_rate(inst[\\\"samples\\\"], subtask))\\n        v_confs.append(vote_fn(inst[\\\"samples\\\"]))\\n\", exact_match_rate, voting_confidence\\nMetrics: Spearman rho, Kendall tau, AUROC (for NER and RE separately)\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import Counter\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom scipy.stats import spearmanr, kendalltau\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, \\\"/root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/code\\\")\\n\\nfrom consistency import compute_all_consistency_scores\\nfrom evaluation import per_instance_f1\\n\\n\\ndef load_jsonl(path):\\n    instances = []\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            if line.strip():\\n                instances.append(json.loads(line))\\n    return instances\\n\\n\\ndef auroc(scores, labels):\\n    scores = np.array(scores, dtype=float)\\n    labels = np.array(labels, dtype=int)\\n    if len(set(labels)) < 2:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    pos = scores[labels == 1]\\n    neg = scores[labels == 0]\\n    if len(pos) == 0 or len(neg) == 0:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    count = sum(\\n        float(np.sum(p > neg) + 0.5 * np.sum(p == neg)) for p in pos\\n    )\\n    return count / (len(pos) * len(neg))\\n\\n\\ndef _entity_set_key(sample):\\n    return frozenset((e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"]) for e in sample.get(\\\"entities\\\", []))\\n\\ndef _relation_set_key(sample):\\n    return frozenset((r[\\\"head\\\"], r[\\\"tail\\\"], r[\\\"type\\\"]) for r in sample.get(\\\"relations\\\", []))\\n\\ndef exact_match_rate(samples, subtask):\\n    if not samples:\\n        return 0.0\\n    key_fn = _entity_set_key if subtask == \\\"ner\\\" else _relation_set_key\\n    keys = [key_fn(s) for s in samples]\\n    counter = Counter(keys)\\n    return counter.most_common(1)[0][1] / len(samples)\\n\\n\\ndef voting_confidence_ner(samples):\\n    N = len(samples)\\n    if N == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    counter = Counter()\\n    for s in samples:\\n        for e in s.get(\\\"entities\\\", []):\\n            counter[(e[\\\"text\\\"], e[\\\"type\\\"])] += 1\\n    if not counter:\\n        return 0.0\\n    return float(np.mean([c / N for c in counter.values()]))\\n\\ndef voting_confidence_re(samples):\\n    N = len(samples)\\n    if N == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    counter = Counter()\\n    for s in samples:\\n        for r in s.get(\\\"relations\\\", []):\\n            counter[(r[\\\"head\\\"], r[\\\"tail\\\"], r[\\\"type\\\"])] += 1\\n    if not counter:\\n        return 0.0\\n    return float(np.mean([c / N for c in counter.values()]))\\n\\n\\ndef compute_all_signals(instances, subtask):\\n    consistency = compute_all_consistency_scores(instances, subtask=subtask)\\n    sj = consistency[\\\"soft_jaccard\\\"]\\n    fk = consistency[\\\"fleiss_kappa\\\"]\\n\\n    em_rates = []\\n    v_confs = []\\n    logprobs = []\\n    greedy_f1s = []\\n\\n    vote_fn = voting_confidence_ner if subtask == \\\"ner\\\" else voting_confidence_re\\n\\n    for inst in instances:\\n        em_rates.append(exact_match_rate(inst[\\\"samples\\\"], subtask))\\n        v_confs.append(vote_fn(inst[\\\"samples\\\"]))\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.35}}"
